} function _updateChartData(widgetName, data){ // Find chart instances and update their data var map={'agents':['chartUsage','chartTokens'],'content':['chartContentPipeline'],'revenue':['chartSubs','revenueChart']}; var ids=map[widgetName]||[]; ids.forEach(function(id){ var el=document.getElementById(id); if(!el||typeof echarts==='undefined')return; var chart=echarts.getInstanceByDom(el); if(!chart)return; var opt=chart.getOption(); if(!opt||!opt.series)return; // Update series data if(id==='chartUsage'){ var tc=data.today_calls||0,ttc=data.total_calls||0,ag=data.active_agents||0,tag=data.total_agents||0; if(tc+ttc+ag+tag===0){ chart.setOption({graphic:[{type:'text',left:'center',top:'center',style:{text:'No Data',fontSize:14,fill:'var(--dim)'}}]}); } }else if(id==='chartContentPipeline'){ var pp=data.published_posts||0,dp=data.draft_posts||0,pr=data.pending_reviews||0,pc=data.pending_comments||0; if(pp+dp+pr+pc===0){ chart.setOption({graphic:[{type:'text',left:'center',top:'center',style:{text:'No Content Yet',fontSize:14,fill:'var(--dim)'}}]}); }else{ chart.setOption({series:[{data:[pp]},{data:[dp]},{data:[pr]},{data:[pc]}]}); } }else if(id==='chartSubs'){ var as=data.active_subscriptions||0,to2=data.total_orders||0; if(to2===0&&as===0){ chart.setOption({graphic:[{type:'text',left:'center',top:'center',style:{text:'No Data',fontSize:14,fill:'var(--dim)'}}]}); }else{ chart.setOption({graphic:[{style:{text:as.toString()}}],series:[{data:[{value:as},{value:Math.max(0,to2-as)}]}]}); } } }); } function _enterBigScreen(){ _bigScreenActive=true; var btn=document.getElementById('btnBigscreen'); if(btn){btn.textContent='Exit'} var clock=document.getElementById('bigscreen-clock'); if(clock){clock.style.display='inline'} document.body.classList.add('bigscreen'); // Collapse sidebar nodes document.querySelectorAll('.sidebar .ng-body').forEach(function(el){el.classList.add('collapsed')}); // Start clock _updateBigScreenClock(); if(_bigScreenClockTimer) clearInterval(_bigScreenClockTimer); _bigScreenClockTimer=setInterval(_updateBigScreenClock,1000); // Start auto-refresh (10s cycle) if(_bigScreenTimer) clearInterval(_bigScreenTimer); if(instance) instance.resize(); }); } },500); } function _exitBigScreen(){ _bigScreenActive=false; var btn=document.getElementById('btnBigscreen'); if(btn){btn.textContent='Big Screen'} var clock=document.getElementById('bigscreen-clock'); if(clock){clock.style.display='none'} document.body.classList.remove('bigscreen'); // Restore sidebar nodes document.querySelectorAll('.sidebar .ng-body.collapsed').forEach(function(el){el.classList.remove('collapsed')}); // Stop auto-refresh if(_bigScreenTimer){clearInterval(_bigScreenTimer);_bigScreenTimer=null;} if(_bigScreenClockTimer){clearInterval(_bigScreenClockTimer);_bigScreenClockTimer=null;} // Resize all charts after DOM settles setTimeout(function(){ if(typeof echarts!=='undefined'){ document.querySelectorAll('#mc .dash-card [id^=chart],#mc .dash-card [id^=health]').forEach(function(el){ var instance=echarts.getInstanceByDom(el); if(instance) instance.resize(); }); } },500); } function toggleBigScreen(){ if(_bigScreenActive){ // Exit big screen if(document.fullscreenElement){ document.exitFullscreen().catch(function(){}); }else{ _exitBigScreen(); } }else{ // Enter big screen var el=document.documentElement; if(el.requestFullscreen){ el.requestFullscreen().catch(function(){ // Fallback: just apply CSS class if fullscreen API fails _enterBigScreen(); }); }else if(el.webkitRequestFullscreen){ el.webkitRequestFullscreen(); }else if(el.msRequestFullscreen){ el.msRequestFullscreen(); }else{ // Browser doesn't support Fullscreen API _enterBigScreen(); } } } // Listen for fullscreen change events document.addEventListener('fullscreenchange',function(){ if(document.fullscreenElement){ _enterBigScreen(); }else{ _exitBigScreen(); } }); document.addEventListener('webkitfullscreenchange',function(){ if(document.webkitFullscreenElement){ _enterBigScreen(); if(!q)return; var result=document.getElementById("matTestChatResult"); result.textContent=""; fetch("/admin/agent-matrix/chat/stream",{ method:"POST", headers:{"Authorization":"Bearer "+T,"Content-Type":"application/json"}, body:JSON.stringify({message:q,history:[]}) }).then(function(r){ var reader=r.body.getReader(); var decoder=new TextDecoder(); function read(){ reader.read().then(function(result){ var text=decoder.decode(result.value,{stream:!result.done}); var lines=text.split("\\n"); lines.forEach(function(line){ line=line.trim(); if(!line||!line.startsWith("data: "))return; var payload=line.slice(6); if(payload=="[DONE]")return; try{ var data=JSON.parse(payload); if(data.role)return; result.textContent+=data; }catch(e){} }); if(!result.done)read(); }); } read(); return null; }); } // ============================================================ // Discussion Mode — Multi-Agent Collaborative Chat (v2.0) // ============================================================ var aiDiscussStartTime = null; var aiDiscussionController = null; // Phase labels for progress indicator var aiDiscussPhaseLabels = { 'planning': 'Planning', 'review': 'Reviewing', 'revision': 'Revising', 'decision': 'Deciding', 'execution': 'Executing' }; function aiDiscussSend() { var inp = document.getElementById('aiChatInput'); var msg = inp.value.trim(); if (!msg) return; inp.value = ''; var msgs = document.getElementById('aiChatMsgs'); msgs.innerHTML += '
👤 Me: ' + esc(msg) + '
'; // Progress indicator with elapsed time aiDiscussStartTime = Date.now(); var progressHTML = '
' + '🤝 Discussion starting... (0.0s)
'; msgs.innerHTML += progressHTML; msgs.scrollTop = msgs.scrollHeight; aiDiscussionController = new AbortController(); fetch('/admin/agent-matrix/chat/discuss', { method: 'POST', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json', 'Authorization': 'Bearer ' + T }, body: JSON.stringify({ message: msg }), signal: aiDiscussionController.signal }).then(function (response) { aiDiscussionStartStream(response); }).catch(function (err) { if (err.name !== 'AbortError') { console.error('Discussion request failed:', err); var progEl = document.getElementById('discussProgress'); if (progEl) progEl.remove(); msgs.innerHTML += '
❌ Discussion request failed
'; } }); } // Line-by-line SSE reader (Fix #3) function aiDiscussionStartStream(response) { var reader = response.body.getReader(); var decoder = new TextDecoder(); var buffer = ''; var currentEvent = {}; function processLine(line) { if (line === '') { if (currentEvent.data !== undefined) { var dataStr = currentEvent.data; if (dataStr === '[DONE]') { var progEl = document.getElementById('discussProgress'); if (progEl) progEl.remove(); aiDiscussionController = null; aiDiscussStartTime = null; return; } try { var msg = JSON.parse(dataStr); handleDiscussMessage(msg, document.getElementById('aiChatMsgs')); } catch (e) { console.warn('Discussion message parse failed:', e, dataStr); } } currentEvent = {}; return; } if (line.startsWith('data: ')) { currentEvent.data = (currentEvent.data || '') + line.slice(6); } else if (line.startsWith('data:')) { currentEvent.data = (currentEvent.data || '') + line.slice(5); } else if (line.startsWith('event: ')) { currentEvent.event = line.slice(7); } } function readChunk() { reader.read().then(function (result) { if (result.done) return; buffer += decoder.decode(result.value, { stream: true }); var pos = 0; var idx; while ((idx = buffer.indexOf('\n', pos)) !== -1) { var line = buffer.substring(pos, idx).replace(/\r$/, ''); pos = idx + 1; processLine(line); } buffer = buffer.substring(pos); readChunk(); }).catch(function (err) { if (err.name !== 'AbortError') { console.error('Discussion stream error:', err); var progEl = document.getElementById('discussProgress'); if (progEl) progEl.remove(); aiDiscussionController = null; aiDiscussStartTime = null; } }); } readChunk(); } function handleDiscussMessage(msg, msgs) { var progressEl = document.getElementById('discussProgress'); switch (msg.type) { case 'phase': var elapsed = aiDiscussStartTime ? ((Date.now() - aiDiscussStartTime) / 1000).toFixed(1) : '...'; if (progressEl) { var label = aiDiscussPhaseLabels[msg.phase] || msg.content; progressEl.textContent = '🤝 ' + label + ' (' + elapsed + 's)'; } break; case 'message': var roleLabel = msg.role || msg.agent || 'Agent'; var cssClass = 'agent'; if (roleLabel === 'Execution Engine') cssClass = 'system'; if (roleLabel === 'Orchestrator') cssClass = 'system'; msgs.innerHTML += renderDiscussMessage({ agent: msg.agent || 'System', role: roleLabel, content: msg.content, timestamp: msg.timestamp ? (msg.timestamp.toFixed ? msg.timestamp.toFixed(1) + 's' : msg.timestamp) : '' }, cssClass); // Remove progress on first message if (progressEl) progressEl.remove(); break; case 'needs_approval': if (progressEl) progressEl.remove(); // Render raw output first msgs.innerHTML += renderDiscussMessage({ agent: msg.agent, role: msg.role, content: msg.content, timestamp: '' }, 'agent'); // Inject approval panel var approvalHTML = '
' + '
' + '⚠ ' + esc(msg.hint || 'Manual approval required') + '
' + '' + '
' + '' + '' + '
'; msgs.innerHTML += approvalHTML; msgs.scrollTop = msgs.scrollHeight; break; case 'warning': if (progressEl) { progressEl.textContent = '⚠ ' + esc(msg.content); progressEl.style.color = 'var(--warn)'; progressEl.style.borderColor = 'var(--warn)'; progressEl.style.background = 'rgba(210,153,34,0.06)'; } break; case 'error': if (progressEl) progressEl.remove(); msgs.innerHTML += '
' + '❌ ' + esc(msg.content) + '
'; break; case 'done': if (progressEl) progressEl.remove(); aiDiscussStartTime = null; aiDiscussionController = null; break; } msgs.scrollTop = msgs.scrollHeight; } // Role-colored chat bubble colors var aiDiscussRoleColors = { 'Planner': { border: '#58a6ff', bg: 'rgba(88,166,255,0.06)', icon: '📋' }, 'Planner (Revised)': { border: '#58a6ff', bg: 'rgba(88,166,255,0.06)', icon: '📋' }, 'Reviewer': { border: '#d29922', bg: 'rgba(210,153,34,0.06)', icon: '🔍' }, 'Decider': { border: '#3fb950', bg: 'rgba(63,185,80,0.06)', icon: '⚖' }, 'Decision Maker': { border: '#3fb950', bg: 'rgba(63,185,80,0.06)', icon: '⚖' }, 'Fast Mode': { border: '#8b949e', bg: 'rgba(139,148,158,0.06)', icon: '⚡' }, 'Execution Engine': { border: '#f0a020', bg: 'rgba(240,160,32,0.06)', icon: '🚀' }, 'Orchestrator': { border: '#6e7681', bg: 'rgba(110,118,129,0.06)', icon: '🎯' } }; function renderDiscussMessage(msg, cssClass) { var colors = aiDiscussRoleColors[msg.role] || { border: '#8b949e', bg: 'rgba(139,148,158,0.06)', icon: '💬' }; var tagColor = colors.border; var html = '

'; // Content html += '
' + esc(msg.content) + '
'; html += '
'; return html; } // Manual approval: accept and execute with user-provided steps function aiApproveDiscussion() { var stepsText = document.getElementById('approvalSteps').value.trim(); if (!stepsText) { alert('Please enter execution steps as a JSON array.'); return; } var steps; try { steps = JSON.parse(stepsText); if (!Array.isArray(steps)) throw new Error('Expected an array'); } catch (e) { alert('Invalid JSON.
